Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The nonlinear variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 752 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 156 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the nonlinear space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 752 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 156. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a nonlinear algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 76. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 410/4.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the nonlinear decomposition. By minimality, the element a^76 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 410-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
